\doxysubsection*{\+: optional name}
\label{_amgrp60bb650acd8acea0f9baf6aec70c6bba}%
struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__resource}{kunit\+\_\+resource}} -\/ represents a {\itshape test managed resource} @data\+: for the user to store arbitrary data.

@free\+: a user supplied function to free the resource. Populated by kunit\+\_\+resource\+\_\+alloc().

Represents a {\itshape test managed resource}, a resource which will automatically be cleaned up at the end of a test case.

Resources are reference counted so if a resource is retrieved via \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_aed20405001d086d18d0fae6a5d52dc7f}{kunit\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+and\+\_\+get\+\_\+resource()}} or \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ab0d382da9ecb9b8a9de962b67cf2eaa1}{kunit\+\_\+find\+\_\+resource()}}, we need to call \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ac6b6c871985d86448882c58ffed20c15}{kunit\+\_\+put\+\_\+resource()}} to reduce the resource reference count when finished with it. Note that \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a8a21e93180a4d3d280554192bf2271ca}{kunit\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+resource()}} does not require a kunit\+\_\+resource\+\_\+put() because it does not retrieve the resource itself.

Example\+:

.. code-\/block\+:: c

struct kunit\+\_\+kmalloc\+\_\+params \{ size\+\_\+t size; gfp\+\_\+t gfp; \};

static int kunit\+\_\+kmalloc\+\_\+init(struct kunit\+\_\+resource $\ast$res, void $\ast$context) \{ struct kunit\+\_\+kmalloc\+\_\+params $\ast$params = context; res-\/$>$data = kmalloc(params-\/$>$size, params-\/$>$gfp);

if (!res-\/$>$data) return -\/E\+N\+O\+M\+EM;

return 0; \}

static void kunit\+\_\+kmalloc\+\_\+free(struct kunit\+\_\+resource $\ast$res) \{ kfree(res-\/$>$data); \}

void $\ast$kunit\+\_\+kmalloc(struct kunit $\ast$test, size\+\_\+t size, gfp\+\_\+t gfp) \{ struct kunit\+\_\+kmalloc\+\_\+params params;

params.\+size = size; params.\+gfp = gfp;

return kunit\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+resource(test, kunit\+\_\+kmalloc\+\_\+init, kunit\+\_\+kmalloc\+\_\+free, \&params); \}

Resources can also be named, with lookup/removal done on a name basis also. \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a7bec535f463cd6532c36a1c4f27117cc}{kunit\+\_\+add\+\_\+named\+\_\+resource()}}, \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ac8f252fd34edce3c0259acac1e933b3a}{kunit\+\_\+find\+\_\+named\+\_\+resource()}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ada48aeb87eaa6ecbdcf60e293a16cd55}{kunit\+\_\+destroy\+\_\+named\+\_\+resource()}}. Resource names must be unique within the test instance. \begin{DoxyCompactItemize}
\item 
\#define \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a9616b7fa88f0c558d29eb80a2abe30ca}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+L\+O\+G\+\_\+\+S\+I\+ZE}}~512

\doxysubsection*{\+: the name of the test case.}
\label{_amgrpe5f344c41900d3802c86432431c2a989}%
struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__case}{kunit\+\_\+case}} -\/ represents an individual test case.

@run\+\_\+case\+: the function representing the actual test case.

@generate\+\_\+params\+: the generator function for parameterized tests.

A test case is a function with the signature, {\ttfamily void ($\ast$)(struct kunit $\ast$)} that makes expectations and assertions (see \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a0dcf3441be7037d8702d620899bf33fa}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+U\+E()}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a70677d585b744aceaf5bff3b54da1ff7}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+E\+R\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+U\+E()}}) about code under test. Each test case is associated with a \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__suite}{kunit\+\_\+suite}} and will be run after the suite\textquotesingle{}s init function and followed by the suite\textquotesingle{}s exit function.

A test case should be static and should only be created with the \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_aaca946803acc3c7d8eba3bc770b50055}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+S\+E()}} macro; additionally, every array of test cases should be terminated with an empty test case.

Example\+:

.. code-\/block\+:: c

void add\+\_\+test\+\_\+basic(struct kunit $\ast$test) \{ \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ace9d5a2f9510ee6a6456ba5cb70a7847}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+E\+Q(test, 1, add(1, 0))}}; \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ace9d5a2f9510ee6a6456ba5cb70a7847}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+E\+Q(test, 2, add(1, 1))}}; K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+EQ(test, 0, add(-\/1, 1)); \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ace9d5a2f9510ee6a6456ba5cb70a7847}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+E\+Q(test, I\+N\+T\+\_\+\+M\+A\+X, add(0, I\+N\+T\+\_\+\+M\+A\+X))}}; K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+EQ(test, -\/1, add(\+I\+N\+T\+\_\+\+M\+A\+X, I\+N\+T\+\_\+\+M\+I\+N)); \}

static struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__case}{kunit\+\_\+case}} example\+\_\+test\+\_\+cases\mbox{[}\mbox{]} = \{ \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_aaca946803acc3c7d8eba3bc770b50055}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+S\+E(add\+\_\+test\+\_\+basic)}}, \{\} \}; \begin{DoxyCompactItemize}
\item 
\#define \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_aaca946803acc3c7d8eba3bc770b50055}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+SE}}(\mbox{\hyperlink{test__bpf_8c_a76fcd1caa086afc31cd997b701e88090}{test\+\_\+name}})~\{ .run\+\_\+case = \mbox{\hyperlink{test__bpf_8c_a76fcd1caa086afc31cd997b701e88090}{test\+\_\+name}}, .\mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2sound_2asoc_8h_a78bcb3acbead9fa4b43530e418cc0b05}{name}} = \#\mbox{\hyperlink{test__bpf_8c_a76fcd1caa086afc31cd997b701e88090}{test\+\_\+name}} \}
\item 
\#define \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a35b182bf3c6aee46e1b3c819cabbbc33}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+S\+E\+\_\+\+P\+A\+R\+AM}}(\mbox{\hyperlink{test__bpf_8c_a76fcd1caa086afc31cd997b701e88090}{test\+\_\+name}},  gen\+\_\+params)

\doxysubsection*{\+: the name of the test. Purely informational.}
\label{_amgrpe1f1bed64323641d1f3733e20b4ee6aa}%
struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__suite}{kunit\+\_\+suite}} -\/ describes a related collection of \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__case}{kunit\+\_\+case}}

@init\+: called before every test case. @exit\+: called after every test case. @test\+\_\+cases\+: a null terminated array of test cases.

A \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__suite}{kunit\+\_\+suite}} is a collection of related \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__case}{kunit\+\_\+case}} s, such that @init is called before every test case and @exit is called after every test case, similar to the notion of a {\itshape test fixture} or a {\itshape test class} in other unit testing frameworks like J\+Unit or Googletest.

Every \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__case}{kunit\+\_\+case}} must be associated with a \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__suite}{kunit\+\_\+suite}} for K\+Unit to run it. \begin{DoxyCompactItemize}

\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a33b68ed5bdccc54ce7d674ca82712e8c}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+E\+R\+T\+\_\+\+E\+Q()}} -\/ Sets an assertion that @left and @right are equal. \begin{DoxyRefDesc}{Test}
\item[\mbox{\hyperlink{test__test000054}{Test}}]\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} test context object. @left\+: an arbitrary expression that evaluates to a primitive C type. @right\+: an arbitrary expression that evaluates to a primitive C type.\end{DoxyRefDesc}


Sets an assertion that the values that @left and @right evaluate to are equal. This is the same as \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ace9d5a2f9510ee6a6456ba5cb70a7847}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+E\+Q()}}, except it causes an assertion failure (see \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a70677d585b744aceaf5bff3b54da1ff7}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+E\+R\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+U\+E()}}) when the assertion is not met. \mbox{\Hypertarget{test_8h_a1a500c44dad3f98449871eb291da3013}\label{test_8h_a1a500c44dad3f98449871eb291da3013}}

\item[\mbox{\hyperlink{test__test000063}{Test}}]\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} test context object. @left\+: an arbitrary expression that evaluates to a null terminated string. @right\+: an arbitrary expression that evaluates to a null terminated string.\end{DoxyRefDesc}


Sets an expectation that the values that @left and @right evaluate to are not equal. This is semantically equivalent to K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+E\+R\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+UE( \begin{DoxyRefDesc}{Test}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+E\+R\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+UE(\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{}]{\mbox{\hyperlink{globtest_8c_a938891c80a62258436f9aaf4bfd38c76}{test}},  }\item[{}]{condition }\end{DoxyParamCaption})~\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a29c09b33e23efc56f78634b9f136c893}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+U\+E\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+E\+R\+T\+I\+ON}}(\mbox{\hyperlink{globtest_8c_a938891c80a62258436f9aaf4bfd38c76}{test}}, \mbox{\hyperlink{assert_8h_afb13bebd1f686b8446d96bbabd748fc7aa9f23a672996ffa8d64d83ba429aa1db}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+E\+R\+T\+I\+ON}}, condition)}

\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a70677d585b744aceaf5bff3b54da1ff7}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+E\+R\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+U\+E()}} -\/ Sets an assertion that @condition is true. \begin{DoxyRefDesc}{Test}
\item[\mbox{\hyperlink{test__test000052}{Test}}]\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} test context object. @condition\+: an arbitrary boolean expression. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} test fails and aborts when this does not evaluate to true.\end{DoxyRefDesc}


This and assertions of the form {\ttfamily K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+E\+R\+T\+\_\+$\ast$} will cause the test case to fail {\itshape and immediately abort} when the specified condition is not met. Unlike an expectation failure, it will prevent the test case from continuing to run; this is otherwise known as an {\itshape assertion failure}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{test_8h_a988e5f7ff5594ae01959c22b82113755}\label{test_8h_a988e5f7ff5594ae01959c22b82113755}}

K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+SE -\/ A helper for creating a \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__case}{kunit\+\_\+case}}

@test\+\_\+name\+: a reference to a test case function.

Takes a symbol for a function representing a test case and creates a \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__case}{kunit\+\_\+case}} object from it. See the documentation for \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__case}{kunit\+\_\+case}} for an example on how to use it. \mbox{\Hypertarget{test_8h_a35b182bf3c6aee46e1b3c819cabbbc33}\label{test_8h_a35b182bf3c6aee46e1b3c819cabbbc33}}

K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+S\+E\+\_\+\+P\+A\+R\+AM -\/ A helper for creation a parameterized \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__case}{kunit\+\_\+case}}

@test\+\_\+name\+: a reference to a test case function. @gen\+\_\+params\+: a reference to a parameter generator function.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} generator function\+:\+:

const void$\ast$ gen\+\_\+params(const void $\ast$prev, char $\ast$desc)

is used to lazily generate a series of arbitrarily typed values that fit into a void$\ast$. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} argument @prev is the previously returned value, which should be used to derive the next value; @prev is set to N\+U\+LL on the initial generator call. When no more values are available, the generator must return N\+U\+LL. Optionally write a string into @desc (size of K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+P\+A\+R\+A\+M\+\_\+\+D\+E\+S\+C\+\_\+\+S\+I\+ZE) describing the parameter. \mbox{\Hypertarget{test_8h_a204ca9232db5c4f7ce43fc8fec69863b}\label{test_8h_a204ca9232db5c4f7ce43fc8fec69863b}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+UE(\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{}]{\mbox{\hyperlink{globtest_8c_a938891c80a62258436f9aaf4bfd38c76}{test}},  }\item[{}]{condition }\end{DoxyParamCaption})~\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a29c09b33e23efc56f78634b9f136c893}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+U\+E\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+E\+R\+T\+I\+ON}}(\mbox{\hyperlink{globtest_8c_a938891c80a62258436f9aaf4bfd38c76}{test}}, \mbox{\hyperlink{assert_8h_afb13bebd1f686b8446d96bbabd748fc7a2a42484edfe340922b7e703affcc4d7b}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+A\+T\+I\+ON}}, condition)}

\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a0dcf3441be7037d8702d620899bf33fa}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+U\+E()}} -\/ Causes a test failure when the expression is not true. \begin{DoxyRefDesc}{Test}
\item[\mbox{\hyperlink{test__test000028}{Test}}]\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} test context object. @condition\+: an arbitrary boolean expression. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} test fails when this does not evaluate to true.\end{DoxyRefDesc}


This and expectations of the form {\ttfamily K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+$\ast$} will cause the test case to fail when the specified condition is not met; however, it will not prevent the test case from continuing to run; this is otherwise known as an {\itshape expectation failure}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{test_8h_a7c3e828647c870a514756490c0eb01e9}\label{test_8h_a7c3e828647c870a514756490c0eb01e9}}

\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_acd8a28cd718f950a8021518b58085fee}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+F\+A\+I\+L()}} -\/ Always causes a test to fail when evaluated. \begin{DoxyRefDesc}{Test}
\item[\mbox{\hyperlink{test__test000027}{Test}}]\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} test context object. @fmt\+: an informational message to be printed when the assertion is made. ...\+: string format arguments.\end{DoxyRefDesc}


\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} opposite of \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ae44c91724563027761cd6c90978bce9e}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+S\+U\+C\+C\+E\+E\+D()}}, it is an expectation that always fails. In other words, it always results in a failed expectation, and consequently always causes the test case to fail when evaluated. See \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a0dcf3441be7037d8702d620899bf33fa}{K\+U\+N\+I\+T\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+T\+R\+U\+E()}} for more information. \mbox{\Hypertarget{test_8h_a020cef9ad6e48bd3911a472f5d0d9d39}\label{test_8h_a020cef9ad6e48bd3911a472f5d0d9d39}}

\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ac588e5625dfaf15c04360ade11f868f2}{kunit\+\_\+mark\+\_\+skipped()}} -\/ Marks @test\+\_\+or\+\_\+suite as skipped

@test\+\_\+or\+\_\+suite\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} test context object. @fmt\+: A \mbox{\hyperlink{linux_2printk_8h_ac002b290a55c356b2b46a7be61a791d4}{printk()}} style format string.

Marks the test as skipped. @fmt is given output as the test status comment, typically the reason the test was skipped.

Test execution continues after \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ac588e5625dfaf15c04360ade11f868f2}{kunit\+\_\+mark\+\_\+skipped()}} is called. \mbox{\Hypertarget{test_8h_a9990c58af8e81b9e521fe39f86a7deb2}\label{test_8h_a9990c58af8e81b9e521fe39f86a7deb2}}

\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a92343d1db1f1647e84278779311b46a1}{kunit\+\_\+skip()}} -\/ Marks @test\+\_\+or\+\_\+suite as skipped

@test\+\_\+or\+\_\+suite\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} test context object. @fmt\+: A \mbox{\hyperlink{linux_2printk_8h_ac002b290a55c356b2b46a7be61a791d4}{printk()}} style format string.

Skips the test. @fmt is given output as the test status comment, typically the reason the test was skipped.

Test execution is halted after \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a92343d1db1f1647e84278779311b46a1}{kunit\+\_\+skip()}} is called. \mbox{\Hypertarget{test_8h_a0460c6f1e29aaf60fe60f3e930cdfdb0}\label{test_8h_a0460c6f1e29aaf60fe60f3e930cdfdb0}}

\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ae7af17a2043e56cc1ce68e2078501581}{kunit\+\_\+test\+\_\+suites()}} -\/ used to register one or more \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__suite}{kunit\+\_\+suite}} with K\+Unit.

@\+\_\+\+\_\+suites\+: a statically allocated list of \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__suite}{kunit\+\_\+suite}}.

Registers @suites with the test framework. See \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__suite}{kunit\+\_\+suite}} for more information.

When builtin, K\+Unit tests are all run via executor; this is done by placing the array of struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__suite}{kunit\+\_\+suite}} $\ast$ in the .kunit\+\_\+test\+\_\+suites E\+LF section.

An alternative is to build the tests as a module. Because modules do not support multiple initcall()s, we need to initialize an array of suites for a module. \mbox{\Hypertarget{test_8h_a1dba06086f2f8f7a8c71251e166efebc}\label{test_8h_a1dba06086f2f8f7a8c71251e166efebc}}

\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_a8a21e93180a4d3d280554192bf2271ca}{kunit\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+resource()}} -\/ Allocates a {\itshape test managed resource}. \begin{DoxyRefDesc}{Test}
\item[\mbox{\hyperlink{test__test000008}{Test}}]\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} test context object. @init\+: a user supplied function to initialize the resource. @free\+: a user supplied function to free the resource. @internal\+\_\+gfp\+: gfp to use for internal allocations, if unsure, use G\+F\+P\+\_\+\+K\+E\+R\+N\+EL @context\+: for the user to pass in arbitrary data to the init function.\end{DoxyRefDesc}


Allocates a {\itshape test managed resource}, a resource which will automatically be cleaned up at the end of a test case. See \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__resource}{kunit\+\_\+resource}} for an example.

Note\+: K\+Unit needs to allocate memory for a \mbox{\hyperlink{structkunit__resource}{kunit\+\_\+resource}} object. You must specify an @internal\+\_\+gfp that is compatible with the use context of your resource. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

\mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ac6b6c871985d86448882c58ffed20c15}{kunit\+\_\+put\+\_\+resource()}} -\/ When caller is done with retrieved resource, \mbox{\hyperlink{test_8h_ac6b6c871985d86448882c58ffed20c15}{kunit\+\_\+put\+\_\+resource()}} should be called to drop reference count. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} resource list maintains a reference count on resources, so if no users are utilizing a resource and it is removed from the resource list, it will be freed via the associated free function (if any).
